<!DOCTYPE html><html lang="en"><head><meta charset="utf-8">
<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1, viewport-fit=cover">
<title>CAT Prep Atlas — 10-Month Plan</title><link rel="icon" href="data:,">
<link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Fraunces:opsz,wght@9..144,600;9..144,800&family=IBM+Plex+Mono:wght@400;600&family=IBM+Plex+Sans:wght@400;500;600&display=swap" rel="stylesheet">
<style>
:root{--bg:#f4efe3;--card:#fffaf0;--ink:#16213e;--mu:#6b6757;--red:#c8372d;--ln:#d9d0b8;--qa:#c8372d;--di:#1f6f8b;--va:#6a4c93;--ok:#2d7a4f;box-sizing:border-box;padding-top:env(safe-area-inset-top,0px);padding-bottom:env(safe-area-inset-bottom,0px)}
@media (prefers-color-scheme:dark){:root:not([data-theme="light"]){--bg:#12162a;--card:#1a2040;--ink:#f1ecdc;--mu:#a7a58f;--red:#ff6b5e;--ln:#2e3560;--qa:#ff6b5e;--di:#5cc4e0;--va:#b99cf0;--ok:#5fd08f;color-scheme:dark}}
:root[data-theme="dark"]{--bg:#12162a;--card:#1a2040;--ink:#f1ecdc;--mu:#a7a58f;--red:#ff6b5e;--ln:#2e3560;--qa:#ff6b5e;--di:#5cc4e0;--va:#b99cf0;--ok:#5fd08f;color-scheme:dark}
html{scroll-padding-top:env(safe-area-inset-top,0px)}*{box-sizing:border-box}
body{margin:0;background:var(--bg);color:var(--ink);font:15px/1.55 "IBM Plex Sans",system-ui,sans-serif;background-image:linear-gradient(var(--ln) 1px,transparent 1px);background-size:100% 32px;background-attachment:local}
.w{max-width:1000px;margin:0 auto;padding:20px 16px 70px}
.hero{padding:26px 0 18px;border-bottom:3px double var(--ink)}
.k{font:600 11px "IBM Plex Mono",monospace;letter-spacing:.18em;color:var(--red)}
h1{font:800 clamp(34px,6vw,60px)/1 Fraunces,Georgia,serif;margin:8px 0;letter-spacing:-.02em}h1 em{color:var(--red);font-style:normal}
.sub{color:var(--mu);max-width:640px;margin:0}
.stats{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fit,minmax(140px,1fr));gap:10px;margin:18px 0}
.st{background:var(--card);border:1.5px solid var(--ink);padding:10px 12px}.st b{font:800 28px Fraunces,serif;display:block}.st span{font:11px "IBM Plex Mono",monospace;color:var(--mu);text-transform:uppercase}
.bar{height:8px;background:var(--ln);margin-top:6px}.bar i{display:block;height:100%;background:var(--ok)}
nav{position:sticky;top:env(safe-area-inset-top,0px);z-index:9;display:flex;gap:6px;overflow-x:auto;padding:8px 0;background:var(--bg);border-bottom:1px solid var(--ink)}
nav button,.mb button{font:600 12px "IBM Plex Mono",monospace;background:var(--card);color:var(--ink);border:1.5px solid var(--ink);padding:7px 12px;cursor:pointer;white-space:nowrap}
nav button.on,.mb button.on{background:var(--ink);color:var(--bg)}
.mb{display:flex;gap:6px;overflow-x:auto;margin:16px 0}
.pg{display:none}.pg.on{display:block}
.card{background:var(--card);border:1.5px solid var(--ink);padding:16px;margin:14px 0;box-shadow:5px 5px 0 var(--ink)}
h2{font:800 26px Fraunces,serif;margin:0 0 4px}h3{font:600 12px "IBM Plex Mono",monospace;letter-spacing:.1em;text-transform:uppercase;margin:16px 0 6px;color:var(--red)}
.g3{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fit,minmax(260px,1fr));gap:12px}
.sec{border-top:5px solid;padding:10px 12px;background:var(--bg)}.sec.QA{border-color:var(--qa)}.sec.DI{border-color:var(--di)}.sec.VA{border-color:var(--va)}
.sec b{font:600 11px "IBM Plex Mono",monospace;letter-spacing:.1em}.sec ul{margin:6px 0 0;padding-left:18px}
label.t{display:flex;gap:10px;padding:8px 0;border-top:1px dashed var(--ln);cursor:pointer}label.t input{width:20px;height:20px;accent-color:var(--red);flex:none;margin-top:2px}
label.t input:checked+span{text-decoration:line-through;color:var(--mu)}
a{color:var(--di);font-weight:600}.mu{color:var(--mu);font-size:13px}
table{width:100%;border-collapse:collapse;font-size:13px;display:block;overflow-x:auto}td,th{border:1px solid var(--ln);padding:6px 8px;text-align:left;white-space:nowrap}th{font:600 11px "IBM Plex Mono",monospace;background:var(--bg)}
input.n,select,textarea{font:inherit;background:var(--card);color:var(--ink);border:1.5px solid var(--ink);padding:6px;width:100%;min-width:70px}textarea{min-height:120px}
.btn{font:600 12px "IBM Plex Mono",monospace;background:var(--red);color:#fff;border:0;padding:8px 14px;cursor:pointer}
.note{border-left:4px solid var(--red);padding:6px 12px;background:var(--bg);margin:10px 0;font-size:14px}
</style></head><body><div class="w">
<header class="hero"><div class="k">CAT · 10-MONTH · 3 HRS/DAY · 6 DAYS/WEEK</div>
<h1>CAT Prep <em>Atlas</em></h1><p class="sub">A month-by-month map for Quant, DILR and VARC — with checkboxes, mock log and free resources. Your ticks are saved on this device.</p>
<div class="stats"><div class="st"><b id="sP">0%</b><span>Overall done</span><div class="bar"><i id="bP" style="width:0"></i></div></div><div class="st"><b id="sM">M1</b><span>Current month</span></div><div class="st"><b id="sK">0</b><span>Mocks logged</span></div><div class="st"><b id="sB">—</b><span>Best mock %ile</span></div></div></header>
<nav id="nav"></nav>

<section class="pg on" id="p-plan"><div class="mb" id="mb"></div><div id="month"></div></section>

<section class="pg" id="p-road"></section>

<section class="pg" id="p-place"><div class="card" style="padding:0"><iframe id="orig" title="Placement tracker" style="width:100%;height:calc(100vh - 150px);min-height:600px;border:0;background:#fff"></iframe></div><p class="mu">Your original Placement Tracker (Java-first), fully working with all your data and tools.</p></section>

<section class="pg" id="p-routine"><div class="card"><h2>Daily 3-hour routine</h2><p class="mu">Six study days, Sunday is buffer — catch up on missed ticks, revise the error log, or rest.</p>
<table><tr><th>Block</th><th>Time</th><th>What to do</th></tr>
<tr><td>Quant</td><td>60 min</td><td>20 min concept → 30 min practice (10–15 Qs) → 10 min log mistakes</td></tr>
<tr><td>DILR</td><td>60 min</td><td>1 set timed (20–25 min) → solve again untimed → write the trick/logic</td></tr>
<tr><td>VARC</td><td>60 min</td><td>30 min reading (editorial/essay) → 2 RC passages or 10 VA Qs → vocab + summary</td></tr></table>
<h3>Weekly rhythm</h3><table><tr><th>Day</th><th>Focus</th></tr><tr><td>Mon / Thu</td><td>New concept heavy (Quant), 1 DILR set, RC</td></tr><tr><td>Tue / Fri</td><td>Practice heavy: sectional sets, para-jumbles, DI</td></tr><tr><td>Wed / Sat</td><td>Revision + timed sectional (from Month 4) or full mock (from Month 7)</td></tr><tr><td>Sunday</td><td>Buffer + error-log review + weekly reflection</td></tr></table>
<div class="note"><b>Error-log rule:</b> every wrong or guessed question goes in a notebook with: topic, why wrong (concept / calculation / time / silly), and the correct method. Re-attempt after 7 days.</div></div></section>

<section class="pg" id="p-cov"></section><script>const COV={"Quantitative Ability": ["Number system basics, properties, divisibility, factors, HCF/LCM|M1·W1|0", "Last digit, last two digits, trailing zeroes, cyclicity|M1·W1|0", "Remainders, Fermat’s theorem|M1·W1|0", "Euler’s & Wilson’s theorems, simplifying the dividend|M4·W4|1", "Binomial theorem|M4·W4|1", "Base systems, conversions, operations in other bases|M4·W4|1", "Percentages, profit & loss, SI/CI|M1·W2|0", "Ratio & proportion, averages, mixtures & alligation|M1·W3|0", "Mean, median, mode|M1·W3|1", "Partnership (accounts), problems on ages|M1·W3|1", "Square root & cube root|M1·W4|1", "Time-speed-distance, time & work|M2·W1–W2|0", "Linear & quadratic equations, finding roots|M2·W3|0", "Algebraic formulae, higher-degree equations, Descartes’ rule|M2·W3|1", "Inequalities, basic functions|M2·W4|0", "Number of integer solutions|M2·W4|1", "AP/GP/HP, sequence & series, logarithms, surds & indices|M3·W1|0", "Functions & graphs, modulus|M3·W2|0", "Modification of graphs, maxima & minima, smallest value of a max function|M3·W2|1", "Triangles, circles, polygons, mensuration 2D/3D|M3·W3–W4|0", "Lines & angles, similar triangles|M3·W3|1", "Permutation, combination, probability, set theory|M4·W1–W2|0", "Coordinate geometry, trigonometry basics|M4·W3|0", "Trig identities, heights & elevation|M4·W3|1"], "DILR": ["Tables, bar & line graphs, pie charts, caselets|M1–M2|0", "Sets & Venn diagrams|M2|0", "Seating & data arrangement (simple), games, selections, scheduling, networks|M1, M3–M4|0", "Blood relations, family tree|M1|1", "Direction sense, coding-decoding|M1|1", "Clocks & calendars|M2|1", "Series, data sufficiency|M2|1", "Syllogism, statements, assumptions, proposition|M3|1", "Binary logic, puzzles|M3–M4|1", "Data structures, data arrangement (matrix, tree)|M4|1", "Revision of all reasoning types|M5|1"], "VARC": ["Reading comprehension, para-summary, parajumbles / jumbled paragraphs|M2 onward|0", "Para completion & inference, odd sentence|M3|0", "Sentence correction, subject-verb agreement, tenses, parallelism|M1, M4|0", "Vocabulary in context|M1–M4|0", "Parts of speech, articles, prepositions|M1|1", "Types of clauses, phrase modifiers|M2|1", "Fill in the blanks, sentence completion|M2|1", "Idioms, one-word substitution, antonyms, analogies|M3|1", "Foreign words in English, different usage of same word|M4|1", "Verbal logic, verbal reasoning, verbal syllogisms|M4|1", "Mixed rapid-fire of all verbal types|M5|1"]};document.getElementById('p-cov').innerHTML='<div class="card"><h2>Coverage Map</h2><p class="mu">Every topic in the CAT syllabus PDF and where it sits in the 10 months. ➕ Added = was missing before and is now scheduled.</p></div>'+Object.entries(COV).map(([k,a])=>`<div class="card"><h2>${k}</h2><table><tr><th>Syllabus topic</th><th>Scheduled</th><th>Status</th></tr>${a.map(x=>{const[t,w,f]=x.split('|');return `<tr><td>${t}</td><td>${w}</td><td>${f==='1'?'➕ Added':'✔ Already in plan'}</td></tr>`}).join('')}</table></div>`).join('');</script><section class="pg" id="p-bak"></section><script>
const BK=()=>Object.keys(localStorage).filter(k=>k.startsWith('cat_')||k==='msjpmdt10b');
function bakData(){const d={};BK().forEach(k=>d[k]=localStorage.getItem(k));return JSON.stringify({app:'CAT Prep Atlas',saved:new Date().toISOString(),data:d},null,1)}
function bakExport(){const t=bakData(),a=document.createElement('a');a.href=URL.createObjectURL(new Blob([t],{type:'application/json'}));a.download='cat-progress-backup-'+new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10)+'.json';a.click();bakMark();bakDraw()}
function bakMark(){try{localStorage.setItem('cat_lastbak',JSON.stringify(new Date().toLocaleString()));localStorage.setItem('cat_lastbak_ts',String(Date.now()))}catch(e){}bakBar()}
function bakBar(){let b=document.getElementById('bakbar');let t=0;try{t=+(localStorage.getItem('cat_lastbak_ts')||0)}catch(e){}if(!b){b=document.createElement('div');b.id='bakbar';b.style.cssText='position:fixed;right:12px;bottom:12px;z-index:50;background:#b45309;color:#fff;padding:8px 12px;border-radius:10px;font:13px system-ui,sans-serif;cursor:pointer;box-shadow:0 2px 8px rgba(0,0,0,.3)';b.onclick=()=>show('bak');b.textContent='💾 Backup due — tap to save your progress';document.body.appendChild(b)}b.style.display=Date.now()-t>6048e5?'block':'none'}
function bakRestore(t){try{const o=JSON.parse(t);if(!o.data)throw 0;const ks=Object.keys(o.data);ks.forEach(k=>localStorage.setItem(k,o.data[k]));alert('Restored '+ks.length+' saved items. The page will reload.');location.reload()}catch(e){alert('That is not a valid backup file.')}}
function bakFile(inp){const r=new FileReader();r.onload=()=>bakRestore(r.result);if(inp.files[0])r.readAsText(inp.files[0])}
function bakCopy(){const t=bakData();(navigator.clipboard?navigator.clipboard.writeText(t):Promise.reject()).then(()=>{bakMark();alert('Backup copied. Paste it into a note and keep it safe.')}).catch(()=>{document.getElementById('bakbox').value=t;alert('Copy the text from the box below.')})}
function bakDraw(){let l='never';try{l=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('cat_lastbak')||'"never"')}catch(e){}document.getElementById('p-bak').innerHTML='<div class="card"><h2>Full-site backup</h2><p class="mu">One backup saves everything on this site: CAT ticks, mock log, notebook, and your Placement &amp; Java tracker data (code, notes, progress). Clearing browser data, or opening the file in another browser, erases them. Export a backup file now and again every week; to restore, choose that file here.</p><p>Last backup: <b>'+l+'</b></p><p><button class="btn" onclick="bakExport()">⬇ Export backup file</button> <button class="btn" onclick="bakCopy()">📋 Copy backup text</button></p><p>Restore from file: <input type="file" accept=".json" onchange="bakFile(this)"></p><p>Or paste backup text:<br><textarea id="bakbox" rows="5" style="width:100%"></textarea><br><button class="btn" onclick="bakRestore(document.getElementById(\'bakbox\').value)">Restore from pasted text</button></p></div>'}
bakDraw();bakBar();</script><section class="pg" id="p-syl"><div id="syl"></div></section>

<section class="pg" id="p-mock"><div class="card"><h2>Mock test log</h2><p class="mu">From Month 4, give 1 sectional a week; from Month 7, 2 full mocks a week. Always analyse for 2× the test time.</p>
<div class="g3"><input class="n" id="mN" placeholder="Mock name"><input class="n" id="mQ" type="number" placeholder="QA score"><input class="n" id="mD" type="number" placeholder="DILR score"><input class="n" id="mV" type="number" placeholder="VARC score"><input class="n" id="mT" type="number" placeholder="Total score"><input class="n" id="mP" type="number" placeholder="Percentile"></div><p><button class="btn" onclick="addMock()">Add mock</button></p>
<table id="mt"></table></div></section>

<section class="pg" id="p-res"><div id="res"></div></section>

<section class="pg" id="p-note"><div class="card"><h2>Notebook</h2><p class="mu">Formulas, tricks, weekly reflection.</p><textarea id="nb" placeholder="Write here…"></textarea></div></section>
</div>
<script>
const S={get(k,d){try{const v=localStorage.getItem('cat_'+k);return v?JSON.parse(v):d}catch(e){return d}},set(k,v){try{localStorage.setItem('cat_'+k,JSON.stringify(v))}catch(e){}}};
const M=[
{n:'Foundation: Arithmetic & Reading Habit',g:'Rebuild school maths and build the reading habit. No timing pressure yet.',
QA:['Number system, divisibility, HCF/LCM','Percentages, profit & loss, SI/CI','Ratio, proportion, averages (mean, median, mode), mixtures & alligation, partnership, ages','Speed calculation: tables to 30, squares to 30, square & cube roots, fractions'],
DI:['Basic tables, bar & line charts','Percentage and ratio-based DI','Simple arrangements (linear, circular)','Blood relations & family trees','Direction sense & coding-decoding'],
VA:['Read 1 editorial daily (The Hindu / Indian Express)','Vocabulary: 10 words/day from reading','Grammar I: parts of speech, articles, subject-verb agreement, tenses, parallelism','Grammar II: prepositions and common errors'],
W:['Finish number system + 150 practice Qs','Finish percentages, P&L, SI/CI + 150 Qs','Ratio, averages, mixtures + 150 Qs','Month test: 40 Qs, 60 min (arithmetic)'],M:'Diagnostic: take the free official CAT mock once to find your baseline.'},
{n:'Arithmetic II & Algebra Begins',g:'Finish all arithmetic; start algebra; first RC practice.',
QA:['Time-speed-distance, trains, boats, races','Time & work, pipes, efficiency','Linear & quadratic equations, algebraic identities, higher-degree equations, Descartes’ rule of signs','Inequalities, basic functions, count of integer solutions'],
DI:['Pie charts, caselets','Tables with missing data','Venn/set-based DI (2–3 sets)','Clocks & calendars','Number/letter series & data sufficiency'],
VA:['RC: 1 passage/day, untimed, note the main idea','Para-summary: 5/week','Parajumbles: intro, 3 Qs/day','Grammar III: types of clauses & phrase modifiers','Fill in the blanks & sentence completion (5/day)'],
W:['TSD + Time&Work, 150 Qs','Equations + inequalities, 150 Qs','Caselets + tables, 8 sets','Month test: 40 Qs QA + 2 DI sets + 3 RC'],M:'Re-take 1 old paper section; compare accuracy with Month 1.'},
{n:'Algebra, Geometry & Logic Basics',g:'Cover the second-largest Quant block and learn how DILR sets are cracked.',
QA:['Progressions (AP/GP/HP) & series, logarithms, surds & indices','Functions & graphs (incl. transformations of graphs), modulus, maxima & minima, smallest value of a max function','Geometry: lines & angles, triangles (similarity, area), circles, polygons','Mensuration: 2D & 3D'],
DI:['Games & tournaments','Selections & distributions','Scheduling & sequencing','Syllogisms, statements, assumptions & propositions','Binary logic (true/false, knights & knaves) and puzzles'],
VA:['RC: 2 passages/day, timed 8 min each','Para-completion & odd-sentence-out','Vocabulary: 15 words/day, flashcards','Idioms & phrases, one-word substitution','Antonyms & analogies'],
W:['Algebra complete, 200 Qs','Geometry: triangles & circles, 150 Qs','Mensuration + coordinate geometry, 100 Qs','Month test: QA 30 Qs/40 min + 2 DILR sets'],M:'Start an error-log notebook if not done already.'},
{n:'Modern Maths, Counting & Sectional Tests',g:'Finish the whole Quant syllabus and start timed sectionals.',
QA:['Permutation, combination, probability','Set theory, Venn diagrams','Coordinate geometry & trigonometry (T-values, identities, heights-and-elevation)','Number System II: Euler’s & Wilson’s theorems, binomial theorem, base systems & conversions, harder remainders'],
DI:['Network/route problems','Mixed-format sets','Maximum/minimum logic sets','Data arrangement & data structures (matrix, tree, tabular layouts)','Mixed puzzles: number, word, cryptarithm'],
VA:['RC: 3 passages/day, mix of topics (science, economy, philosophy)','Vocabulary in context','Verbal ability: sentence correction, odd-one-out','Foreign words used in English; different usages of the same word','Verbal logic & verbal reasoning (inference, syllogism-style conclusions)'],
W:['P&C and probability, 150 Qs','Set theory + coordinate geometry/trigonometry, 100 Qs','Number System II (Euler, Wilson, bases, binomial), 100 Qs','Syllabus complete: final revision list, then 1st sectional tests (QA; DILR + VARC)'],M:'First sectionals: aim to attempt 70% of questions; accuracy matters.'},
{n:'Practice Mode: Speed & Accuracy',g:'Timed practice every day. Build on strengths, repair weak spots.',
QA:['Topic-wise 25-Q timed sets','Previous-year questions by topic','Shortcut and approximation drills','Weekly weak-topic revision'],
DI:['2 sets/day, 40 min total','Learn to pick sets (skip trap sets)','Rapid skim, then decide in 3 minutes','Rapid-fire revision of all reasoning types (blood relations to data sufficiency)'],
VA:['RC: 4 passages/day timed','Inference & tone-based questions','Weekly speed-reading challenge','Mixed VA rapid-fire: blanks, idioms, analogies, antonyms, clauses, prepositions, articles'],
W:['Weekly sectional: QA','Weekly sectional: DILR','Weekly sectional: VARC','Analyse trends: topic-wise accuracy sheet'],M:'Target: 60%+ accuracy in every section.'},
{n:'Advanced Topics & Strategy',g:'Tackle hardest questions and develop section strategy.',
QA:['Advanced number theory, remainders','Hard geometry, mixed Qs','Quant sections 20 Q in 40 min','Previous 5 years of CAT QA'],
DI:['Hard-level sets, 3 per week','Multi-layered logic grids','Arrangements with conditions'],
VA:['RC: long passages, abstract topics','Para-jumbles with 5 sentences','Daily newspaper summary in 3 lines'],
W:['Hardest Quant practice','Weekly sectional + 90-min analysis','Solve 2 previous-year DILR slots','Strategy note: which sets to attempt/skip'],M:'Decide your section order: strongest first usually works.'},
{n:'Full Mock Season Begins',g:'2 full mocks a week; analysis matters more than scores.',
QA:['Targeted topics from mock mistakes','2 sectionals/week','Previous-year slots','Formula revision sheet'],
DI:['Set selection practice','Solve 1 set fully, 1 half-attempt','Time-box each set to 20 min'],
VA:['RC accuracy over speed','Daily 2 RC + 5 VA Qs','Revise vocabulary weekly'],
W:['2 mocks + analysis','2 mocks + analysis','2 mocks + analysis','2 mocks + month review'],M:'Mock scores should stabilise; log each in the Mock tab.'},
{n:'Weakness Repair & Mock Analysis',g:'Convert errors into marks; build stamina.',
QA:['Redo all error-log questions','Revise formulas daily','Topic tests on weakest 3 areas','Timed 30-min sets'],
DI:['Re-solve failed sets untimed','Attempt 4 sets in 2 hours at test time','Learn to abandon bad sets'],
VA:['RC: only difficult passages','Revise grammar rules','Read 2 long-form essays/week'],
W:['2 mocks, analysis, error log','2 mocks, analysis, error log','2 mocks, analysis, error log','Percentile review'],M:'Check if percentile is rising; adjust target.'},
{n:'Peak Mock Phase',g:'Exam simulation at the actual slot time. Maintain health and sleep.',
QA:['Light daily formula revision','Mock-based topics only','Final tricks sheet','Avoid new topics'],
DI:['Daily 2 sets, timed','Review mistakes by pattern','Attempt strategy rehearsal'],
VA:['Read daily 30 min','RC practice from recent mocks','Vocabulary quick revision'],
W:['3 mocks/week + analysis','3 mocks/week + analysis','3 mocks/week + analysis','Final strategy review'],M:'Keep sleep fixed; avoid burnout.'},
{n:'Final Month: Polish & Calm',g:'Stay sharp, not stressed. No new concepts.',
QA:['Revise formula sheet','Revisit error-log','2 sectionals/week','Rest on Sundays'],
DI:['1 set/day timed','Review old solved sets','Light practice before exam'],
VA:['Daily reading','1 RC set/day','Revisit grammar notes'],
W:['2 mocks + light revision','1 mock + error review','Rest + revision','Exam day checklist, admit card, centre visit'],M:'Exam checklist: ID, admit card, sleep, travel plan.'}];
const R=[['Quant','NCERT Maths (Class 6–10), free at ncert.nic.in','https://ncert.nic.in/textbook.php'],['Quant','Khan Academy: arithmetic, algebra, geometry','https://www.khanacademy.org/math'],['Quant','2IIM free CAT lessons (YouTube)','https://www.youtube.com/@2IIMCAT'],['Quant/DILR','Cracku: free CAT lessons & practice (YouTube)','https://www.youtube.com/@CrackuTV'],['DILR','Previous-year CAT DILR sets: practise by topic','https://www.google.com/search?q=CAT+previous+year+DILR+sets+pdf'],['Mocks','Official free CAT mock: iimcat.ac.in','https://iimcat.ac.in'],['VARC','The Hindu editorial page','https://www.thehindu.com/opinion/editorial/'],['VARC','Aeon essays (long-form reading)','https://aeon.co/essays'],['VARC','Indian Express opinion','https://indianexpress.com/section/opinion/'],['Strategy','InsideIIM: CAT strategy and experience articles','https://insideiim.com']];
const SYL={QA:['Arithmetic (~45%)|Percentages, P&L, ratio, TSD, time & work, mixtures, SI/CI, averages','Algebra (~25%)|Equations, inequalities, progressions, functions, logs, surds','Geometry & Mensuration (~15%)|Triangles, circles, polygons, coordinate geometry, 2D/3D','Number System (~10%)|Divisibility, remainders, factors, base systems','Modern Maths (~5%)|P&C, probability, set theory'],DI:['Data Interpretation|Tables, charts, caselets, mixed-format','Logical Reasoning|Arrangements, grids, games & tournaments, networks, scheduling, selections'],VA:['Reading Comprehension (~16 Qs)|Inference, main idea, tone, detail, application','Verbal Ability (~8 Qs)|Parajumbles, para-summary, odd sentence, para-completion']};
const NV=['plan|📅 CAT Plan','road|🗓 240-Day Roadmap','place|🎯 Placement & Java','routine|⏱ Daily Routine','syl|📚 Syllabus Map','cov|✅ Coverage Map','mock|📊 Mock Log','res|🔗 Free Resources','note|📝 Notebook','bak|💾 Backup'];
const nav=document.getElementById('nav');NV.forEach(v=>{const[i,t]=v.split('|');const b=document.createElement('button');b.textContent=t;b.dataset.p=i;b.onclick=()=>show(i);nav.appendChild(b)});
function show(i){document.querySelectorAll('.pg').forEach(p=>p.classList.toggle('on',p.id==='p-'+i));nav.querySelectorAll('button').forEach(b=>b.classList.toggle('on',b.dataset.p===i))}
show('plan');
let cur=S.get('cur',0);const done=S.get('done',{});
function mbtn(){const mb=document.getElementById('mb');mb.innerHTML='';M.forEach((m,i)=>{const b=document.createElement('button');b.textContent='Month '+(i+1);b.className=i===cur?'on':'';b.onclick=()=>{cur=i;S.set('cur',i);mbtn();rm()};mb.appendChild(b)})}
function ids(i){return ['QA','DI','VA'].map(s=>M[i][s].map((_,j)=>`${i}-${s}-${j}`)).flat().concat(M[i].W.map((_,j)=>`${i}-W-${j}`))}
function rm(){const m=M[cur];const sec=(c,l,a)=>`<div class="sec ${c}"><b>${l}</b>`+`<div>${a.map((x,j)=>`<label class="t"><input type="checkbox" data-id="${cur}-${c}-${j}" ${done[cur+'-'+c+'-'+j]?'checked':''}><span>${x}</span></label>`).join('')}</div></div>`;
document.getElementById('month').innerHTML=`<div class="card"><div class="k">MONTH ${cur+1} OF 10</div><h2>${m.n}</h2><p class="mu">${m.g}</p>
<div class="g3">${sec('QA','QUANT',m.QA)}${sec('DI','DILR',m.DI)}${sec('VA','VARC',m.VA)}</div>
<h3>Weekly targets</h3>${m.W.map((x,j)=>`<label class="t"><input type="checkbox" data-id="${cur}-W-${j}" ${done[cur+'-W-'+j]?'checked':''}><span><b>Week ${j+1}:</b> ${x}</span></label>`).join('')}
<div class="note"><b>Milestone:</b> ${m.M}</div></div>`;
document.querySelectorAll('#month input').forEach(c=>c.onchange=()=>{done[c.dataset.id]=c.checked;S.set('done',done);upd()})}
function upd(){const all=M.flatMap((_,i)=>ids(i));const d=all.filter(x=>done[x]).length;const p=Math.round(100*d/all.length);document.getElementById('sP').textContent=p+'%';document.getElementById('bP').style.width=p+'%';document.getElementById('sM').textContent='M'+(cur+1)}
document.getElementById('syl').innerHTML=Object.entries(SYL).map(([k,a])=>`<div class="card"><h2>${{QA:'Quantitative Ability',DI:'DILR',VA:'VARC'}[k]}</h2>${a.map(x=>{const[t,d]=x.split('|');return `<h3>${t}</h3><div class="mu">${d}</div>`}).join('')}</div>`).join('');
document.getElementById('res').innerHTML='<div class="card"><h2>Free resources</h2><p class="mu">All free. Pick one source per topic and finish it.</p>'+R.map(r=>`<label class="t" style="cursor:default"><span><b>${r[0]}</b> · <a href="${r[2]}" target="_blank" rel="noopener">${r[1]}</a></span></label>`).join('')+'</div>';
let mocks=S.get('mocks',[]);
function addMock(){const g=id=>document.getElementById(id).value;if(!g('mN'))return;mocks.push({n:g('mN'),q:g('mQ'),d:g('mD'),v:g('mV'),t:g('mT'),p:g('mP')});S.set('mocks',mocks);['mN','mQ','mD','mV','mT','mP'].forEach(i=>document.getElementById(i).value='');rk()}
function rk(){document.getElementById('mt').innerHTML='<tr><th>#</th><th>Mock</th><th>QA</th><th>DILR</th><th>VARC</th><th>Total</th><th>%ile</th></tr>'+mocks.map((m,i)=>`<tr><td>${i+1}</td><td>${m.n.replace(/</g,'&lt;')}</td><td>${m.q}</td><td>${m.d}</td><td>${m.v}</td><td>${m.t}</td><td>${m.p}</td></tr>`).join('');document.getElementById('sK').textContent=mocks.length;const b=Math.max(0,...mocks.map(m=>+m.p||0));document.getElementById('sB').textContent=b?b:'—'}
const nb=document.getElementById('nb');nb.value=S.get('nb','');nb.oninput=()=>S.set('nb',nb.value);
mbtn();rm();rk();upd();

JDK = JRE + compiler (javac). Write once, run anywhere.<br>\u2022 Program anatomy: class, public static void main(String[] args); statements end with ;<br>\u2022 Primitives: byte, short, int, long, float, double, char, boolean. String is an object.<br>\u2022 Casting: widening is automatic (int to double); narrowing needs (int) and can lose data.<br>\u2022 Input: Scanner sc = new Scanner(System.in); nextInt(), nextLine(). Output: print, println, printf.<br><b>Watch out<\/b>: nextInt() leaves the newline behind; call nextLine() once before reading a full line.|<b>Tasks<\/b><br>1. Install JDK 21 and IntelliJ; run Hello World.<br>2. Compile one file with javac and run it with java.<br>3. Write: swap two numbers, simple interest, Celsius to Fahrenheit, area of a circle, read name and age and greet.<br>4. Print the max and min of int and double using Integer.MAX_VALUE.<br><b>Check<\/b>: explain JDK vs JRE vs JVM aloud in 30 seconds.\",\n\"<b>Concepts<\/b><br>\u2022 Operators: + - * / % (integer division truncates; % gives the remainder), relational (== != < >), logical (&& || ! with short-circuit), bitwise (& | ^ << >>).<br>\u2022 x++ uses then increments; ++x increments then uses.<br>\u2022 Precedence: * / % before + -; relational before &&; use brackets when unsure.<br>\u2022 if / else if / else, nested conditions, ternary (cond ? a : b).<br>\u2022 switch: case, break, default, fall-through; arrow form (case 1 -> ...) in Java 14+.<br><b>Watch out<\/b>: = assigns, == compares; compare Strings with equals.|<b>Tasks<\/b><br>1. Even or odd, positive/negative/zero, leap year, largest of three.<br>2. Grade calculator (marks to A/B/C/F) with if-else, then redo with switch.<br>3. Menu calculator: read two numbers and an operator, switch on it, handle divide by zero.<br>4. Predict the output of 5 ++/-- puzzles on paper, then run them.<br><b>Check<\/b>: explain short-circuit evaluation with an example.\",\n\"<b>Concepts<\/b><br>\u2022 for (init; condition; update), while (checks first), do-while (runs at least once).<br>\u2022 break exits the loop; continue skips to the next iteration; labelled break for nested loops.<br>\u2022 Nested loops: outer = rows, inner = columns; iterations = rows x columns.<br>\u2022 Digit tricks: n % 10 is the last digit, n / 10 drops it.<br>\u2022 Pattern method: for each row decide the spaces and symbols, then write the formula using the row number.<br><b>Watch out<\/b>: off-by-one errors and infinite loops from a forgotten update.|<b>Tasks<\/b><br>1. Factorial, sum of digits, reverse a number, palindrome number.<br>2. Fibonacci up to n; prime check up to sqrt(n); primes from 1 to 100.<br>3. Ten patterns: right triangle, inverted triangle, pyramid, diamond, number triangle, Floyd's triangle.<br>4. Dry-run one nested loop on paper with a trace table.<br><b>Check<\/b>: explain why a prime check only needs to go up to sqrt(n).\",\n\"<b>Concepts<\/b><br>\u2022 Method = name + parameters + return type + body; void returns nothing.<br>\u2022 Java is always pass-by-value: primitives copy the value; object references copy the address, so changing an object's fields inside a method is visible outside, but reassigning the reference is not.<br>\u2022 Overloading: same name, different parameter list (return type alone is not enough); resolved at compile time.<br>\u2022 Scope: a local variable lives only in its block; fields live with the object or class.<br>\u2022 Recursion: needs a base case and a smaller subproblem.<br><b>Watch out<\/b>: a missing base case gives StackOverflowError.|<b>Tasks<\/b><br>1. MathUtil class: isPrime, gcd, power, reverseNumber, isArmstrong.<br>2. Overload area() for circle, rectangle and triangle.<br>3. Recursion: factorial, Fibonacci, sum of digits, power; draw the call stack for factorial(4).<br>4. Prove with code that swapping two ints inside a method does not change the caller's values.<br><b>Check<\/b>: explain pass-by-value using an object example.\",\n\"<b>Concepts<\/b><br>\u2022 Array: fixed size, one type, zero-indexed; int[] a = new int[5]; a.length; defaults are 0, false, null.<br>\u2022 Traverse with for and for-each; out-of-range index throws ArrayIndexOutOfBoundsException.<br>\u2022 Arrays class: sort, fill, copyOf, copyOfRange, equals, toString, binarySearch (sorted data only).<br>\u2022 2-D arrays: int[][] m = new int[3][4]; row-major traversal; jagged arrays have rows of different lengths.<br>\u2022 Patterns: running max/min, swap from both ends, shifting, frequency array.<br><b>Watch out<\/b>: b = a copies the reference, not the data.|<b>Tasks<\/b><br>1. Max, min, second largest, sum, average.<br>2. Reverse in place; rotate by k; move zeros to the end.<br>3. Matrix: add, multiply, transpose, row sums, column sums, diagonal sum.<br>4. Print a 2-D array with Arrays.deepToString.<br><b>Check<\/b>: write second largest in one pass without sorting.\",\n\"<b>Concepts<\/b><br>\u2022 String is immutable: every change creates a new object; the string pool reuses identical literals.<br>\u2022 == compares references, equals compares content; equalsIgnoreCase; compareTo for ordering.<br>\u2022 Methods: length, charAt, substring, indexOf, lastIndexOf, contains, startsWith, split, replace, trim, toCharArray, toUpperCase.<br>\u2022 StringBuilder is mutable (append, insert, reverse, deleteCharAt); StringBuffer is the thread-safe, slower version.<br>\u2022 Use StringBuilder inside loops; += on Strings creates many objects.<br><b>Watch out<\/b>: new String(x) bypasses the pool, so == can be false.|<b>Tasks<\/b><br>1. Reverse a string two ways (StringBuilder and a char loop).<br>2. Palindrome, vowel and consonant count, remove spaces, count words.<br>3. Anagram check (sort and frequency array), first non-repeating character, character frequency.<br>4. Show with code why == and equals differ for new String objects.<br><b>Check<\/b>: explain immutability and why it helps security and the pool.\",\n\"<b>Concepts<\/b><br>\u2022 Class = blueprint (fields + methods); object = instance created with new, stored on the heap.<br>\u2022 Constructors: same name as the class, no return type; default, parameterised, overloaded; chaining with this(...).<br>\u2022 this refers to the current object and resolves field vs parameter name clashes.<br>\u2022 static belongs to the class and is shared; static methods cannot use instance fields directly; a static block runs once at class load.<br>\u2022 Initialisation order: static block, instance block, constructor.<br><b>Watch out<\/b>: once you write any constructor, Java stops providing the default one.|<b>Tasks<\/b><br>1. Student class (id, name, marks) with a constructor and display().<br>2. BankAccount with deposit and withdraw.<br>3. Counter class that counts created objects with a static variable.<br>4. Print the order of static block, instance block and constructor to confirm it.<br><b>Check<\/b>: explain class vs object and static vs instance aloud.\",\n\"<b>Concepts<\/b><br>\u2022 Encapsulation = private fields + public getters/setters, so the class controls how data changes.<br>\u2022 Access modifiers: private (class), default (package), protected (package + subclasses), public (everywhere).<br>\u2022 Validate in setters: reject negative salary, empty name.<br>\u2022 Packages group classes; import statements; package names are lowercase.<br>\u2022 Immutable class: final class, private final fields, no setters (String is the classic example).<br><b>Watch out<\/b>: returning a mutable field (like an array) from a getter leaks internal state.|<b>Tasks<\/b><br>1. Employee class with private fields, getters, and a setter that rejects salary below 0.<br>2. Split a small project into packages: model, service, main.<br>3. Create an immutable Point class.<br>4. Access a private field from another class and read the compiler error.<br><b>Check<\/b>: write the four access modifiers as a table from memory.\",\n\"<b>Concepts<\/b><br>\u2022 extends gives a subclass the parent's fields and methods (IS-A); Java allows single inheritance for classes.<br>\u2022 super(...) calls the parent constructor (first line); super.method() calls the parent version.<br>\u2022 Overriding: same name and parameters, @Override, access cannot be reduced, return type same or narrower.<br>\u2022 Object class: toString, equals, hashCode; override toString for readable output.<br>\u2022 final: variable cannot change, method cannot be overridden, class cannot be extended.<br><b>Watch out<\/b>: overloading is decided at compile time, overriding at runtime.|<b>Tasks<\/b><br>1. Vehicle with Car and Bike subclasses, each adding a field and overriding describe().<br>2. Shape hierarchy (Circle, Rectangle) with area() overridden.<br>3. Override toString and equals in Student and test both.<br>4. Print the constructor call order for a three-level hierarchy.<br><b>Check<\/b>: state three differences between overloading and overriding.\",\n\"<b>Concepts<\/b><br>\u2022 Polymorphism: one reference type, many behaviours. Compile-time = overloading; runtime = overriding (dynamic dispatch).<br>\u2022 Upcasting: Parent p = new Child(); only parent members are visible, but overridden methods run the child's version. Downcasting needs (Child) p, ideally after instanceof.<br>\u2022 Abstract class: cannot be instantiated; may have abstract and concrete methods, fields, constructors.<br>\u2022 Interface: a contract; a class can implement many; default and static methods since Java 8.<br>\u2022 Use an abstract class for shared state and code, an interface for a capability.<br><b>Watch out<\/b>: a wrong downcast throws ClassCastException.|<b>Tasks<\/b><br>1. Payment interface with UPI, Card and NetBanking classes; one method that accepts any Payment.<br>2. Abstract Shape with Circle, Square, Triangle stored in one Shape[]; print every area.<br>3. Show an interface default method.<br>4. Write a 5-row table: abstract class vs interface.<br><b>Check<\/b>: explain runtime polymorphism with the Payment example.\",\n\"<b>Day 1: plan and start<\/b><br>\u2022 Write requirements: check balance, deposit, withdraw, change PIN, exit.<br>\u2022 Classes: Account (balance, pin), ATM (menu loop), Main.<br>\u2022 Sketch the menu flow on paper before coding.<br>\u2022 Build the menu with a while loop and switch.<br>\u2022 Implement deposit, withdraw (reject if balance is insufficient) and balance check.<br>\u2022 Run it and fix bugs.<br><b>Goal by end of day<\/b>: a working console menu with three operations.|<b>Day 2: finish<\/b><br>1. Add a PIN check with 3 attempts.<br>2. Validate input (negative amounts, non-numbers) so the program never crashes.<br>3. Add a mini statement (last 5 transactions) using an array or list.<br>4. Write README.md: what it does, how to compile and run, a sample session.<br><b>Check<\/b>: explain your class design in 2 minutes as if in an interview.\",\n\"<b>Day 1: attempt<\/b><br>\u2022 Solve 15 HackerRank Java problems (Basics, Strings, OOP) with no hints, at most 20 minutes each.<br>\u2022 Keep a mistakes log: problem, what went wrong, the concept.<br>\u2022 Mark every concept you had to look up.<br><b>Goal<\/b>: finish all 15 and have a list of weak concepts.|<b>Day 2: repair<\/b><br>1. Redo every wrong problem from scratch.<br>2. For each weak concept, re-read your notes and write one new example.<br>3. Explain the four OOP pillars (encapsulation, inheritance, polymorphism, abstraction) aloud with examples from your own code.<br>4. Write 10 Java interview questions you can now answer.<br><b>Check<\/b>: record your explanation and listen back for gaps.\"]];\nconst How Java works<\/h3><p>You write source code in a <b>.java<\/b> file. The compiler <b>javac<\/b> turns it into <b>bytecode<\/b> (a <b>.class<\/b> file). The <b>JVM<\/b> runs that bytecode on any operating system, which is why Java is called write once, run anywhere.<\/p><table><tr><th>Term<\/th><th>What it is<\/th><\/tr><tr><td>JVM<\/td><td>Runs bytecode<\/td><\/tr><tr><td>JRE<\/td><td>JVM + standard libraries (needed to run programs)<\/td><\/tr><tr><td>JDK<\/td><td>JRE + javac + tools (needed to write programs)<\/td><\/tr><\/table>\n<h3>2. Setup<\/h3><ul><li>Install JDK 21 (an LTS version) and IntelliJ IDEA Community or VS Code.<\/li><li>In a terminal run <b>java -version<\/b> and <b>javac -version<\/b>; both must print a version.<\/li><\/ul>\n<h3>3. Your first program<\/h3><pre class='cd'>public class Hello {\n    public static void main(String[] args) {\n        System.out.println(\"Hello, Java!\");\n    }\n}<\/pre><ul><li><b>public class Hello<\/b>: if the class is public, the file must be named Hello.java.<\/li><li><b>main<\/b> is where execution starts. <b>static<\/b> lets the JVM call it without an object; <b>String[] args<\/b> holds command-line arguments.<\/li><li><b>System.out.println<\/b> prints text and moves to a new line.<\/li><li>Statements end with <b>;<\/b>. Java is case-sensitive: String is not string.<\/li><\/ul><p>Run it: <b>javac Hello.java<\/b> then <b>java Hello<\/b>.<\/p>\n<h3>4. Primitive data types<\/h3><table><tr><th>Type<\/th><th>Size<\/th><th>Holds<\/th><\/tr><tr><td>byte<\/td><td>8 bit<\/td><td>-128 to 127<\/td><\/tr><tr><td>short<\/td><td>16 bit<\/td><td>-32768 to 32767<\/td><\/tr><tr><td>int<\/td><td>32 bit<\/td><td>about -2.1 billion to 2.1 billion (default for whole numbers)<\/td><\/tr><tr><td>long<\/td><td>64 bit<\/td><td>very large whole numbers; write 5L<\/td><\/tr><tr><td>float<\/td><td>32 bit<\/td><td>decimals; write 2.5f<\/td><\/tr><tr><td>double<\/td><td>64 bit<\/td><td>decimals (default for decimals)<\/td><\/tr><tr><td>char<\/td><td>16 bit<\/td><td>one character in single quotes: 'A'<\/td><\/tr><tr><td>boolean<\/td><td>true or false<\/td><td>conditions<\/td><\/tr><\/table><p>Default values of fields: 0 for numbers, false for boolean, null for objects. Local variables have no default and must be assigned before use. <b>String<\/b> is not primitive; it is a class and uses double quotes.<\/p>\n<h3>5. Variables and naming<\/h3><pre class='cd'>int age = 20;\ndouble price = 99.5;\nchar grade = 'A';\nboolean passed = true;\nString name = \"Asha\";\nfinal double PI = 3.14159;   // final = cannot change<\/pre><ul><li>Names may contain letters, digits, _ and $, cannot start with a digit and cannot be a keyword.<\/li><li>Convention: variables camelCase, classes PascalCase, constants UPPER_CASE.<\/li><\/ul>\n<h3>6. Type conversion<\/h3><pre class='cd'>int i = 10;\ndouble d = i;          // widening, automatic: 10.0\nint x = (int) 7.9;     // narrowing, explicit cast: 7 (truncates)\nint big = Integer.MAX_VALUE + 1;  // overflow: becomes negative<\/pre><p>Order of widening: byte, short, int, long, float, double. Narrowing can lose data, so Java forces you to write the cast.<\/p>\n<h3>7. Input and output<\/h3><pre class='cd'>import java.util.Scanner;\n\npublic class Input {\n    public static void main(String[] args) {\n        Scanner sc = new Scanner(System.in);\n        System.out.print(\"Name: \");\n        String name = sc.nextLine();\n        System.out.print(\"Age: \");\n        int age = sc.nextInt();\n        System.out.printf(\"%s is %d years old%n\", name, age);\n        sc.close();\n    }\n}<\/pre><ul><li>printf codes: <b>%d<\/b> int, <b>%.2f<\/b> decimal with 2 places, <b>%s<\/b> String, <b>%c<\/b> char, <b>%n<\/b> new line.<\/li><li>nextInt() leaves the Enter key in the buffer. If you call nextLine() after it, you get an empty line; call sc.nextLine() once to clear it first.<\/li><\/ul>\n<h3>8. Worked solutions to the tasks<\/h3><pre class='cd'>// Swap two numbers\nint a = 5, b = 3;\nint t = a; a = b; b = t;       // a=3, b=5\n\n// Simple interest\ndouble p = 1000, r = 5, years = 2;\ndouble si = p * r * years / 100;   // 100.0\n\n// Celsius to Fahrenheit\ndouble c = 37;\ndouble f = c * 9 / 5 + 32;         // 98.6<\/pre>\n<h3>9. Common mistakes<\/h3><ul><li>Missing semicolon or brace: the compiler error points near the line.<\/li><li>Class name and file name do not match for a public class.<\/li><li>Writing 7/2 and expecting 3.5; it is 3 for ints. Use 7/2.0.<\/li><li>Using == to compare Strings; use equals.<\/li><\/ul>`;\nconst fl=(a,b,c)=>{const f=FN[a+\"_\"+(b*3+(c>>1))];return f?`<details open><summary><b>\ud83d\udcd8 Full lists~1~That is a collection.~Each entry is a key with a value.~That is a set.~It is not sorted.\",\n\"What chooses the bucket?~The key's hashCode~The value's length~Insertion duplicates.~A repeat add is ignored.~Null is one value.~Type does not matter.\",\n\"Which keeps keys sorted?~HashMap~LinkedHashMap~TreeMap~HashSet~2~HashMap has no order.~It keeps insertion order.~TreeMap sorts by key.~HashSet has no order.\",\n\"Which keeps insertion order?~HashMap~LinkedHashMap~TreeMap~None~1~HashMap has no guaranteed order.~LinkedHashMap remembers insertion order.~TreeMap sorts instead.~One does.\",\n\"Average get() time in a HashMap?~O(n)~O(1)~O(log n)~O(n log n)~1~That is the worst case.~Hashing gives constant time on average.~That is TreeMap.~Not typical.\",\n\"Two keys landing in the same bucket is called?~Overflow~Collision~Exception~Deadlock~1~Overflow is different.~It is a hash collision.~No exception occurs.~Deadlock is about threads.\",\n\"What does getOrDefault(k,0) do?~Throws if missing~Returns 0 if k is absent~Adds k~Deletes k~1~It does not throw.~It gives the default for missing keys.~It does not add.~It does not delete.\",\n\"Why keep equals and hashCode consistent?~For sorting~Equal keys must find the same entry~For printing~For threads~1~Sorting is separate.~Lookup relies on both.~Printing uses toString.~Threads are unrelated.\",\n\"TreeMap get() complexity?~O(1)~O(log n)~O(n)~O(n^2)~1~That is HashMap.~It is a balanced tree.~That is a plain list.~It is not quadratic.\"],[\n\"What do generics add?~Speed~Compile-time type safety~Inheritance~Threads~1~They do not speed code.~The compiler checks element types.~Inheritance is separate.~Threads are unrelated.\",\n\"Which method does Comparable define?~compare~compareTo~equals~sort~1~That belongs to Comparator.~compareTo defines natural order.~equals is on Object.~sort is not a method of it.\",\n\"Which method does Comparator define?~compareTo~compare~order~sort~1~That belongs to Comparable.~compare(a, b) defines custom order.~order is not a method.~sort is not it.\",\n\"Modifying a list inside for-each throws?~ConcurrentModificationException~NullPointerException~IOException~StackOverflowError~0~The iterator detects the change.~Nothing is null.~No I/O happens.~No recursion happens.\",\n\"What is a safe way to remove while iterating?~list.remove in the loop~Iterator.remove()~list.clear()~Not possible~1~That throws.~Iterator.remove is designed for it.~That empties everything.~It is possible.\",\n\"What does T extends Number mean?~Any type~Number or a subclass~A constant~A final type~1~That would be plain T.~T is bounded to Number types.~It is not a constant.~It is not final.\",\n\"Can you write List<int>?~Yes~No, use Integer~Yes in Java 21~Only for arrays~1~Generics need object types.~Use the wrapper class Integer.~Java 21 still forbids it.~Arrays are different.\",\n\"How does Collections.sort order Strings by default?~Alphabetical~Random~By length~Reversed~0~String natural order is alphabetical.~It is deterministic.~Length is not used.~Not reversed.\",\n\"How do you sort by two fields?~Comparator.comparing(...).thenComparing(...)~Sort twice by hand~Arrays.sort2~Only one field is possible~0~thenComparing chains the second key.~That is clumsy and wrong.~Arrays.sort2 does not exist.~Chaining is possible.\",\n\"When are generics checked?~Compile time, erased at runtime~Only at runtime~Never~At linking~0~Type erasure removes them later.~They are compile-time.~They are checked.~Linking is unrelated.\"]];\nFN[\"0_1\"]=`<h3>1. Operators<\/h3><table><tr><th>Group<\/th><th>Operators<\/th><\/tr><tr><td>Arithmetic<\/td><td>+ - * / %<\/td><\/tr><tr><td>Relational<\/td><td>== != &lt; &gt; &lt;= &gt;=<\/td><\/tr><tr><td>Logical<\/td><td>&amp;&amp; || !<\/td><\/tr><tr><td>Assignment<\/td><td>= += -= *= /= %=<\/td><\/tr><tr><td>Unary<\/td><td>++ -- + - !<\/td><\/tr><tr><td>Bitwise<\/td><td>&amp; | ^ ~ &lt;&lt; &gt;&gt;<\/td><\/tr><\/table><pre class='cd'>7 / 2      // 3     integer division drops the fraction\n7 % 2      // 1     remainder\n7 / 2.0    // 3.5   one double makes the result double\n-7 % 3     // -1    the sign follows the left number\n\nint a = 5;\nint x = a++;   // x = 5, then a = 6  (use, then increase)\nint y = ++a;   // a = 7, y = 7       (increase, then use)\n\n5 &amp; 3  // 1   (101 and 011 = 001)\n5 | 3  // 7   (111)\n5 ^ 3  // 6   (110, bits that differ)\n5 &lt;&lt; 1 // 10  (shift left = multiply by 2)\n5 &gt;&gt; 1 // 2   (shift right = divide by 2)<\/pre><p><b>Precedence, high to low:<\/b> brackets, ++ -- !, * / %, + -, relational, == !=, &amp;&amp;, ||, ternary, assignment. Use brackets when unsure.<\/p><p><b>Short-circuit:<\/b> in A &amp;&amp; B, B is skipped if A is false; in A || B, B is skipped if A is true. Example: <b>x != 0 &amp;&amp; 10 / x &gt; 1<\/b> never divides by zero.<\/p>\n<h3>2. Conditions<\/h3><pre class='cd'>int marks = 72;\nif (marks &gt;= 90) {\n    System.out.println(\"A\");\n} else if (marks &gt;= 60) {\n    System.out.println(\"B\");\n} else {\n    System.out.println(\"C\");\n}\n\nString result = marks &gt;= 40 ? \"Pass\" : \"Fail\";   // ternary<\/pre><ul><li>The condition must be a boolean. <b>if (x = 5)<\/b> does not compile, because = assigns.<\/li><li>Compare Strings with <b>equals<\/b>, never ==.<\/li><li>Always use braces, even for one line.<\/li><\/ul>\n<h3>3. switch<\/h3><pre class='cd'>int day = 3;\nswitch (day) {\n    case 1: System.out.println(\"Mon\"); break;\n    case 2: System.out.println(\"Tue\"); break;\n    case 3: System.out.println(\"Wed\"); break;\n    default: System.out.println(\"Other\");\n}\n\n// Java 14+ arrow form, no fall-through\nString name = switch (day) {\n    case 1 -&gt; \"Mon\";\n    case 2 -&gt; \"Tue\";\n    default -&gt; \"Other\";\n};<\/pre><ul><li>Without <b>break<\/b> the old form falls through into the next case.<\/li><li>switch works on int, char, String and enum, not on long, float or double.<\/li><\/ul>\n<h3>4. Worked solutions<\/h3><pre class='cd'>// Leap year\nboolean leap = (y % 4 == 0 &amp;&amp; y % 100 != 0) || y % 400 == 0;\n\n// Largest of three\nint max = a;\nif (b &gt; max) max = b;\nif (c &gt; max) max = c;\n\n// Menu calculator\nswitch (op) {\n    case '+': r = a + b; break;\n    case '-': r = a - b; break;\n    case '*': r = a * b; break;\n    case '/': r = (b != 0) ? (double) a / b : 0; break;\n}<\/pre>\n<h3>5. Common mistakes<\/h3><ul><li>Using = instead of ==.<\/li><li>Forgetting break in a switch.<\/li><li>Expecting 7/2 to be 3.5.<\/li><li>Writing a &lt; b &lt; c; Java needs a &lt; b &amp;&amp; b &lt; c.<\/li><\/ul>`;\nlet busy=\"\",err=\"\";\nconst fm=s=>esc(s).replace(/\\*\\*(.+?)\\*\\*/g,\"<b>$1<\/b>\").replace(/\\n/g,\"<br>\");\nconst gn=(a,b,c)=>((ND[a]||[])[b*3+(c>>1)]||N[a][b*3+(c>>1)]).replace(/<br>/g,\"\\n\").replace(/<[^>]+>/g,\"\").replace(/\\|/g,\" | \");\nasync function ai(kind){st.q=st.q||{};st.dn=st.dn||{};const k=K(m,w,d),p=pl(m,w,d),ctx=`Topic: \"${p.t}\" (${p.B?\"second day of the topic: revise and practise\":\"first day of the topic: learn\"}). dynamic-programming problems']},\n{id:'code-review',cat:'Practice',icon:'\ud83d\udd0d',name:'Code Review Checklist',desc:'Review your solution for correctness, clarity and code','Add tests for normal, boundary and invalid inputs']},\n{id:'debug-journal',cat:'Practice',icon:'\ud83d\udc1e',name:'Debugging Journal',desc:'Capture root causes so the same bug does not keep returning.',tasks:['Write the exact error or unexpected behaviour','Record the smallest reproducible input','Identify the root cause, not only the visible symptom','Write the fix and why it works','Add a regression test or prevention rule']},\n{id:'system-design',cat:'Practice',icon:'\ud83c\udfd7\ufe0f',name:'System Design Fundamentals',desc:'Practise entry-level design: requirements, example request/response','Explain one scalability, reliability or security trade-off']},\n{id:'cs-oral',cat:'Practice',icon:'\ud83c\udf93',name:'Core CS Oral Revision',desc:'Practise explaining CS ACID','Explain OOP principles with your own examples','Explain stack, queue, tree, graph and Big-O']},\n{id:'communication',cat:'Interview',icon:'\ud83d\udde3\ufe0f',name:'Communication Practice clarifying questions before solving','Replace filler words with short pauses','Listen back and note one improvement for next time']},\n{id:'question-ask',cat:'Interview',icon:'\u2753',name:'Interviewer Questions Planner',desc:'Prepare thoughtful questions about the role, team and expectations.',tasks:['Prepare a question questions already answered clearly on the company site']},\n{id:'referrals',cat:'Career',icon:'\ud83e\udd1d',name:'Networking & Referral Tracker',desc:'Track professional outreach respectfully and timeline','Draft a concise follow-up message if the timeline passes','Record the response and next action']},\n{id:'interview-calendar',cat:'Career',icon:'\ud83d\uddd3\ufe0f',name:'Interview Day ready']},\n{id:'offer-review',cat:'Career',icon:'\u2696\ufe0f',name:'Offer Comparison Worksheet',desc:'Compare role quality and total compensation without focusing on CTC alone.',tasks:['Record fixed
